Remnant 4.png\"\\\n    \\]\n}\n\nThere is a related tutorial page for this topic!\n\n \n\nSee [Tutorial:Defeating a bastion remnant](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Tutorial:Defeating_a_bastion_remnant \"Tutorial:Defeating a bastion remnant\")\n.\n\n**Bastion remnants**, also simply referred to as **bastions**,[\\[1\\]](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant#cite_note-1)\n are large, castle-like [generated structures](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Generated_structures \"Generated structures\")\n found in [the Nether](https://minecraft.wiki/w/The_Nether \"The Nether\")\n in all of the biomes except [basalt deltas](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Basalt_deltas \"Basalt deltas\")\n. They generate in four distinct variants, each with a unique structure and set of loot. [Piglins](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Piglin \"Piglin\")\n and [piglin brutes](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Piglin_brute \"Piglin brute\")\n spawn in these structures on generation, and [hoglins](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Hoglin \"Hoglin\")\n may also spawn on generation in the bridge and hoglin stables variants. Bastion remnants are the only place to find [gilded blackstone](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Gilded_blackstone \"Gilded blackstone\")\n, the [Pigstep music disc](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Pigstep_music_disc \"Pigstep music disc\")\n, the [snout banner pattern](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Snout_banner_pattern \"Snout banner pattern\")\n and [armor trim](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Snout_Armor_Trim \"Snout Armor Trim\")\n, [monster spawners](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Monster_spawner \"Monster spawner\")\n which spawn [magma cubes](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Magma_cube \"Magma cube\")\n, and the [netherite upgrade](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Netherite_upgrade \"Netherite upgrade\")\n smithing template.\n\nContents\n--------\n\n*   [1 Generation](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant#Generation)\n    \n*   [2 Structure](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant#Structure)\n    *   [2.1 Navigation](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant#Navigation)\n    \n\nGeneration\n----------\n\n\\[[edit](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?section=1&veaction=edit \"Edit section: Generation\")\\\n | [edit source](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?action=edit&section=1 \"Edit section's source code: Generation\")\\\n\\]\n\n[](https://minecraft.wiki/w/File:Java_Nether_Structure_Generation1.png)\n\nNether structure generation in [_Java Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition \"Java Edition\")\n. The black lines represent each region and the dots represent coordinates. Green is where they can generate and red is where they cannot.\n\n[](https://minecraft.wiki/w/File:Bedrock_Nether_Structure_Generation.png)\n\nNether structure generation in [_Bedrock Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bedrock_Edition \"Bedrock Edition\")\n. The black lines represent each region and the dots represent coordinates. Green is where they can generate and red is where they cannot.\n\nBastion remnants generate in all Nether biomes except basalt deltas, although a bastion may extend into one. If a bastion tries to generate in a basalt delta, the bastion does not generate and a [Nether fortress](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Nether_fortress \"Nether fortress\")\n generates instead. To generate the structure, the game splits the Nether into regions, in which either a Nether fortress or a bastion remnant can generate. The regions are 432×432 blocks in [_Java Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition \"Java Edition\")\n and 480×480 blocks in [_Bedrock Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bedrock_Edition \"Bedrock Edition\")\n. Each region has a 4-chunk separation located on the south and east borders of the region in which neither a fortress nor a bastion can generate. This leaves only a 368×368 block section in [_Java Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition \"Java Edition\")\n or 416×416 block section in [_Bedrock Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bedrock_Edition \"Bedrock Edition\")\n where a structure can generate. Two structures never generate in the same region, although they might overlap if they generate close to the separation border. In [_Java Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition \"Java Edition\")\n, the chance of a bastion generating instead of a fortress is 3⁄5 (60%), while in [_Bedrock Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bedrock_Edition \"Bedrock Edition\")\n the chance of a bastion generating instead of a fortress is 2⁄3 (66.6%). Each of the four variants of bastion remnants has an equal chance of generating.\n\nPiglins, piglin brutes, and hoglins that spawn upon generation in bastion remnants neither despawn naturally nor do they hunt each other. Piglin brutes also spawn exclusively here upon structure generation, and do not despawn unless killed, or if [difficulty](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Difficulty \"Difficulty\")\n is set to Peaceful. Like [shulkers](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Shulker \"Shulker\")\n in [end cities](https://minecraft.wiki/w/End_cities \"End cities\")\n, once these mobs are killed, they do not respawn, though the bastion remnant continues to spawn normal mobs depending on the biome it is located in.\n\nStructure\n---------\n\n\\[[edit](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?section=2&veaction=edit \"Edit section: Structure\")\\\n | [edit source](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?action=edit&section=2 \"Edit section's source code: Structure\")\\\n\\]\n\nMain article: [Bastion Remnant/Structure](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant/Structure \"Bastion Remnant/Structure\")\n\nBastion remnants generate as 4 types of structures: bridges, hoglin stables, housing units, and treasure rooms. Each type has its own set of structures that compose it. These structures may be supported by ramparts that contain cave-like paths. [Piglins](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Piglin \"Piglin\")\n, [piglin brutes](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Piglin_brute \"Piglin brute\")\n, and [hoglins](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Hoglin \"Hoglin\")\n spawn frequently throughout. Unlike [Nether fortresses](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Nether_fortress \"Nether fortress\")\n, when over the [lava sea](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Lava_sea \"Lava sea\")\n, they do not generate pillars of solid blocks down to the solid terrain beneath, but float above, entirely even.[\\[2\\]](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant#cite_note-2)\n\nAll bastion remnant structures found below are located in the folder [client.jar](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Client.jar \"Client.jar\")\n/data/minecraft/structures/bastion.\n\n### File structure\n\n\\[[edit](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?section=3&veaction=edit \"Edit section: File structure\")\\\n | [edit The inside of the \"mouth\" consists of multiple levels of walkways surrounded by lava. Bricks\") | [](https://minecraft.wiki/w/File:Polished_Blackstone_Bricks_JE2_BE2.png) |\n\n### Treasure room\n\n\\[[edit](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?section=7&veaction=edit \"Edit section: Treasure room\")\\\n | [edit source](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?action=edit&section=7 \"Edit section's source code: Treasure room\")\\\n\\]\n\nThe **treasure room** structure consists of a large rampart supported by buttresses, with a bridge going between it and the treasure room itself. The rampart contains several treasure chests and a handful of [blocks of gold](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Blocks_of_gold \"Blocks of gold\")\n.\n\nThe treasure room consists of many bridges suspended above a lava floor. At the bottom of a treasure room, there is a [magma cube](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Magma_cube \"Magma cube\")\n monster spawner, and a center loot area consisting of [blocks of gold](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Blocks_of_gold \"Blocks of gold\")\n, as well as 1 or 2 treasure chests. Additional chests generate in the bastion remnant, but they use the \"generic\" loot table instead of the \"treasure\" loot table. Treasure room pieces are contained in the subfolder treasure.\n\n[Ghasts](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Ghast \"Ghast\")\n may spawn in the treasure room if the room is in the correct biome, which can create a difficult experience for the player. Their group size depends on the biome and edition.\n\n| Structure name | Description | Consists of | \\[show\\]Image |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `bastion/treasure/bases/centers/center_0` | Center loot areas on the lava basin in the treasure room. [1.16](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_1.16 \"Java Edition 1.16\") |     |     | [20w16a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_20w16a \"Java Edition 20w16a\") |     |     |     | Added bastion remnants. |\n| [20w17a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_20w17a \"Java Edition 20w17a\") |     |     |     | Bastion remnant loot has been tweaked. |\n| Ancient debris and netherite scrap have been made rarer. |\n| Diamond gear is now found instead of netherite gear. |\n| Damaged randomly enchanted crossbows can now be found in bastion bridge chests. |\n| Other small changes to weights, stack sizes, pool contents, and rolls have been made. |\n| [Barriers](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Barrier \"Barrier\")<br> no longer generate in bastion lava pools.[\\[3\\]](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant#cite_note-42) |\n| [20w19a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_20w19a \"Java Edition 20w19a\") |     |     |     | Bastion remnants are now slightly less common. |\n| [pre3](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_1.16-pre3 \"Java Edition 1.16-pre3\") |     |     |     | Duplicate, unused structures have been removed. |\n| The fifth inner and outer large hoglin stable variants did not generate prior to this pre-release. |\n| A stray [emerald block](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Emerald_block \"Emerald block\")<br> has been removed from `treasure/big_air_full`. This emerald block was never found in naturally generated bastions as it would be overwritten. |\n| [1.16.2](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_1.16.2 \"Java Edition 1.16.2\") |     |     | [20w27a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_20w27a \"Java Edition 20w27a\") |     |     |     | Added [piglin brutes](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Piglin_brutes \"Piglin brutes\")<br>, which spawn in bastion remnants. |\n| [20w30a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_20w30a \"Java Edition 20w30a\") |     |     |     | [Gilded blackstone](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Gilded_blackstone \"Gilded blackstone\")<br> is now more likely to generate underneath [chests](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Chest \"Chest\")<br> in bastion remnants. |\n| The loot of bastion remnant chests has been changed, generally making more valuable items more common and in larger quantities. |\n| [1.18.2](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_1.18.2 \"Java Edition 1.18.2\") |     |     | [pre1](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_1.18.2-pre1 \"Java Edition 1.18.2-pre1\") |     |     |     | If a bastion attempts to generate in basalt deltas, a [Nether fortress](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Nether_fortress \"Nether fortress\")<br> generates in its place, instead of being skipped entirely. |\n| [1.19.4](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_1.19.4 \"Java Edition 1.19.4\")<br><br>Experiment<br><br>[Update 1.20](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Trails_%26_Tales \"Trails & Tales\") |     |     | [23w04a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_23w04a \"Java Edition 23w04a\") |     |     |     | [Netherite upgrades](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Netherite_upgrade \"Netherite upgrade\")<br> and [snout armor trims](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Snout_armor_trim \"Snout armor trim\")<br> are now found in bastion chests. |\n| [23w05a](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition_23w05a \"Java Edition 23w05a\") |     |     |     | Increased the chance of [netherite upgrades](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Netherite_upgrade \"Netherite upgrade\")<br> and [snout armor trims](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Snout_armor_trim \"Snout armor trim\")<br> from 3.2% to 10% and from source](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?action=edit&section=20 \"Edit section's source code: Issues\")\\\n\\]\n\nIssues relating to \"Bastion Remnant\" are maintained on the [bug tracker](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bug_tracker \"Bug tracker\")\n. Issues should be reported and viewed [there](https://bugs.mojang.com/issues/?jql=project%20in%20%28MC%2C%20MCPE%29%20AND%20%28resolution%20is%20EMPTY%20OR%20resolution%20in%20%281%2C%202%2C%206%29%29%20AND%20%28summary%20~%20%22Bastion%20Remnant%22%29%20ORDER%20BY%20resolution%20DESC)\n.\n\nTrivia\n------\n\n\\[[edit](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?section=21&veaction=edit \"Edit section: Trivia\")\\\n | [edit source](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?action=edit&section=21 \"Edit section's source code: Trivia\")\\\n\\]\n\n[](https://minecraft.wiki/w/File:Abandoned_jigsaw_test_1.png)\n\n`bastion/jigsaw_test` in _Bedrock Edition_. The structure has been modified to replace the invalid `orientation` states with `north_up`.\n\n[](https://minecraft.wiki/w/File:Abandoned_jigsaw_test_2.png)\n\nSigns in `bastion/jigsaw_test`.\n\n*   The structure `bastion/hoglin_stable/starting_pieces/stairs_1_mirrored`, despite containing the word \"mirrored\", it is not stored as mirrored like the other mirrored stairs. This has no effect on generation due to the placement of [jigsaw blocks](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Jigsaw_block \"Jigsaw block\")\n     in the structure.\n*   In [_Bedrock Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bedrock_Edition \"Bedrock Edition\")\n    , there are multiple unused structure files. Unlike the unused structures in [_Java Edition_](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Java_Edition \"Java Edition\")\n    , these structures are more technical.\n    *   Two files named `bastion/units/wall_units/edge_0`, and `bastion/treasure/ramparts/lava_basin` contain blocks that have not been added to the game, including `blackstone_bricks`, `blackstone_brick_stairs`, `cracked_blackstone_bricks`, and `chiseled_blackstone_bricks`.\n    *   A test structure file, `bastion/jigsaw_test`, contains two oak signs, some jigsaw blocks, stained glass, and stone.\n        *   All of the jigsaw blocks in this structure use invalid `orientation` states: `identity`, `rot_90_x_neg`, `rot_120_npp`, `rot_120_nnn`, `rot_180_edge_yz_pos`, `rot_90_x_pos`, `rot_120_ppn`, `rot_120_pnp`, `rot_180_edge_yz_neg`, `rot_180_face_xz`, `rot_90_y_neg`, `rot_90_y_pos`\n*   Buried sections can exist within the bastion and alongside it. Ore may generate alongside or within [netherrack](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Netherrack \"Netherrack\")\n    , but [glowstone blobs](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Glowstone_blob \"Glowstone blob\")\n     can do so within its air.\n\nGallery\n-------\n\n\\[[edit](https://minecraft.wiki/w/Bastion_Remnant?section=22&veaction=edit \"Edit section: Gallery\")\\\n | [edit
